*Kai Van Brunt,Justin Kay,Sara Beery*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于多模态大语言模型（MLLM）的交互式预测校正工作流Molmo2Fish，用于声纳鱼类追踪场景，通过人机对话方式修正不完美的跟踪预测，并在引导/非引导任务中评估其性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 计算机视觉越来越多地用于大规模生态数据集的自动化识别任务，但多目标跟踪等复杂任务仍具挑战性。研究者探索利用人在回路（human-in-the-loop）流程使不完美的预测变得有用，但现有方法缺乏灵活高效的交互方式，需要新的交互式预测校正机制。

Method: 提出一个以多模态大语言模型为媒介的交互式预测校正工作流，将预测修正视为与模型的对话过程。该工具名为Molmo2Fish，针对声纳鱼类跟踪数据集进行定制。研究中分别在引导（guided）和非引导（unguided）任务下评估工具对自身预测轨迹和外部轨迹的修正能力。

Result: Molmo2Fish在鱼类跟踪和轨迹修正任务上取得了较高的性能，但将自然语言引导纳入模型的潜力仍未充分挖掘，仍有很大的改进空间。

Conclusion: 以多模态大语言模型为驱动的对话式预测纠错在生态学视觉任务中展现出可行性，但进一步提升性能需要更好地整合自然语言引导能力。代码和数据已公开。

Abstract: Computer vision is increasingly used to automate recognition tasks in large ecological datasets, but more complex tasks such as multi-object tracking continue to pose challenges. As researchers seek to incorporate vision models in ecology workflows, various lines of research have explored how to make imperfect predictions useful through human-in-the-loop processes. We propose a new approach to working with imperfect tracking predictions through an interactive prediction correction workflow taking place as a conversation with a multimodal large language model, which we tailor to a sonar fish tracking dataset as an initial proof of concept. We investigate the performance of the tool, Molmo2Fish, across guided and unguided tasks, correcting its own predicted tracks and external tracks. We find that Molmo2Fish achieves high performance on fish tracking and track correction tasks, but there is still much room to improve on incorporating natural language guidance. The code and data are publicly available at https://github.com/tidalove/molmo2fish.

</details>


### [34] [VA-Judger: Reward Modeling from Human Preference Feedback for Joint Video-Audio Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18607)
*Yinming Huang,Shuyuan Tu,Xi Yan,Zihan Yang,Jianhua Han,Xu Hang,Yu-Gang Jiang,Zuxuan Wu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了VA-Judger，一个用于联合视频-音频生成模型的思维链全方位奖励模型，通过构造大规模人类偏好数据集VAPref-10K和VA-Judger-Bench基准，采用分阶段训练策略（先学清晰质量差距的粗粒度判别，再通过拒绝采样蒸馏难样本的偏好解释，最后进行维度级强化学习），以解决现有指标无法捕捉文本-视频-音频整体语义和时间一致性以及激励奖励黑客的问题，实验证明其在人类偏好预测和生成质量提升上均优于现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有强化学习后训练联合视频-音频生成模型时使用的奖励信号由各质量维度（音频质量、视觉保真度、同步性）的独立指标组合而成，但这些指标分离地评估感知维度，无法捕捉文本提示、视频和音频之间影响人类偏好的整体语义和时间一致性。优化这些指标会鼓励奖励黑客行为，生成在指标上得分高但人类看来不连贯或不忠实的视频-音频内容。

Method: (1) 构建VAPref-10K人类偏好数据集，包含9K提示和10.3K细粒度成对比较，来源于开源生成模型；(2) 引入VA-Judger-Bench基准，包含域内和域外模型比较以评估奖励模型是否真正对齐人类偏好；(3) 提出VA-Judger思维链全方位奖励模型，分三阶段训练：先学习具有清晰质量差距的样本对以建立结构化输出和粗粒度偏好判别；然后通过拒绝采样（经人类标注验证）蒸馏难样本的可靠偏好解释；最后进行维度级强化学习，将人类反馈分解为各个质量维度，提供比单一二值偏好标签更密集的奖励信号。

Result: 实验表明VA-Judger在域内和域外评估中预测人类偏好均优于指标基线。使用其对人类对齐的奖励对音频-视频生成模型进行后训练，生成质量也获得了显著提升。

Conclusion: VA-Judger通过人类偏好对齐的思维链奖励模型有效解决了联合视频-音频生成中奖励黑客问题，其分阶段训练策略和维度级强化学习方法能够提供更密集且与人类判断一致的奖励信号，从而在奖励建模和下游生成质量两方面都取得显著提升。

Abstract: Using reinforcement learning to post-train joint video-audio generation models requires a reward signal. Existing methods construct this reward by combining metrics for individual quality dimensions, including audio quality, visual fidelity, and synchronization. However, these metrics evaluate perceptual dimensions separately and fail to capture the overall semantic and temporal coherence among the text prompt, video, and audio that shapes human preferences. Optimizing models against these metrics encourages reward hacking, generating video-audio content that achieves high scores on these metrics yet appears incoherent or unfaithful to human viewers. To address this problem, we first construct a large-scale human-preference dataset VAPref-10K for joint video-audio generation, comprising 9K prompts and 10.3K fine-grained paired comparisons from open-source generation models. We also introduce the VA-Judger-Bench benchmark with both in-domain and out-of-domain model comparisons to evaluate whether reward models truly align with human preferences. We further propose VA-Judger, a chain-of-thought omni-reward model for joint video-audio generation. In particular, VA-Judger first learns from pairs with clear quality gaps to establish structured output and coarse preference discrimination, then distills reliable preference explanations for harder near-quality comparisons via rejection sampling verified against human annotations, and finally performs dimension-wise reinforcement learning that decomposes human feedback into individual quality dimensions for denser reward signals than a single binary preference label. Experiments show that VA-Judger outperforms metric baselines in predicting human preferences on both in-domain and out-of-domain evaluations. Using its human-aligned rewards for post-training audio-video generation model also yields significant improvements in generation quality.

</details>


### [35] [CDGP: Contrastive Dual Gaussian Processes for Weakly Supervised Anomaly Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18614)
*Seungjun Chu,Seokhee Han,Mateusz Nowak,Peter Chin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出CDGP（对比双高斯过程），一种弱监督异常分割框架，通过建模正常与异常诱导变量的预测分布，利用后验优势统计量（标准差化的预测均值差除以联合预测不确定性）提供空间证据与图像级置信度，在MVTec AD~2、KSDD2和VisA上取得领先性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业视觉检测需要同时判定产品是否有缺陷并定位缺陷，但像素级掩码标注成本高昂。现有大多数异常分割方法仅使用无缺陷图像学习并评分偏离正常程度的区域，然而真实缺陷与异常但正常的区域都可能显著偏离正常分布并得到相似的高分，导致误检。因此需要一种无需像素标注、能更好区分真实缺陷与正常偏差的弱监督方法。

Method: CDGP将正常和异常两类诱导变量的预测分布建模为高斯过程，核心创新后验优势统计量通过将预测均值之差异标准化（除以联合预测不确定性）来提供空间证据和图像级置信度。该证据与分层正常重建残差互补，用于精细定位。所有校准仅使用训练数据，无需人工像素标注或测试时拟合。作者还通过分解和匹配的线性头对照实验来界定线性核高斯过程公式化表述的贡献与适用范围。

Result: 在MVTec AD~2、KSDD2和VisA三个数据集上，CDGP在MVTec AD~2的所有定位指标中排名第一，在KSDD2和VisA上取得第一名或具有竞争力。

Conclusion: CDGP通过弱监督方式有效解决了异常分割中真实缺陷与正常偏差难以区分的问题，后验优势统计量与正常重建残差的互补机制在多个基准上验证了其有效性，且避免了昂贵的像素级标注依赖。

Abstract: Industrial visual inspection must both decide whether a product is defective and localize the defect, yet pixel-level masks are costly to collect at scale. Most anomaly-segmentation methods learn only from defect-free images and score deviations from normality. A true defect and an unusual-but-normal region, however, can both deviate substantially and receive similarly high scores. We propose Contrastive Dual Gaussian Processes (CDGP), a weakly supervised framework that models normal and anomaly inducing-variable predictive distributions over dense tokens. Its posterior-dominance statistic standardizes their predictive-mean difference by the joint predictive uncertainty, providing both spatial evidence and image-level confidence. Across MVTec AD~2, KSDD2, and VisA, CDGP ranks first among the evaluated methods on all MVTec AD~2 localization metrics and is first-place or competitive on KSDD2 and VisA. Factorized and matched linear-head controls delimit the contribution and scope of the linear-kernel Gaussian process (GP) formulation.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Conclusion: SPARC流水线实现了胎儿心脏MRI 3D+time电影重建的全自动化处理，在速度、质量和可靠性方面均优于现有方法，满足临床部署需求；该流水线已作为Docker容器公开发布，并已在所在机构作为临床研究工具实际部署使用。

Abstract: Fetal cardiac MRI (fCMR) provides valuable diagnostic information complementary to echocardiography, particularly for complex congenital heart disease (CHD). Dynamic cine imaging captures cardiac motion essential for assessment of cardiac function; however, the reconstruction of 3D+time cine volumes from 2D+time acquired slices remains challenging due to unpredictable fetal motion and the absence of automated and robust processing tools suitable for clinical deployment. We present the SPARC pipeline (Slice-to-volume Pipeline for Automated Reconstruction of gated 3D+time fetal Cardiac MRI) which combines physics-informed slice-to-volume reconstruction (SVR) of Doppler ultrasound (DUS) gated stacks of slices, assisted by deep learning (DL) models for thoracic segmentation and anatomical reorientation. The proposed SVR algorithm achieves a tenfold reduction in reconstruction time relative to existing frame-wise approaches ($4.8 \pm 1.0$ vs $49.0 \pm 14.1$ min, $p < 0.0001$) while improving the reconstruction quality. Thoracic segmentation performance using ensemble aggregation exceeded inter-rater agreement (Dice $84.7 \pm 3.9\%$ vs $81.4 \pm 7.7\%$, $p<0.05$), while anatomical reorientation achieved a success rate of $90.1\%$. End-to-end evaluation on a large held-out clinical cohort ($n = 121$) demonstrated fully automatic processing in $82.6\%$ of cases with a mean runtime of $7.1 \pm 1.3$ min, compatible with clinical deployment. The complete SPARC pipeline is publicly available as a Docker container https://hub.docker.com/r/aboutill/sparc and is currently deployed at our institution as a clinical research tool.

### [37] [PALATE: Personalized Aesthetic Learning through Adaptive Taste Evolution for Multi-User Portrait Retouching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18622)
*Jingxuan Wang,Yifan Mei,Yuxia Niu,Chaowan Jiao,Qijin Shen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出PALATE框架，在保持图像编辑器固定不变的前提下，通过共享奖励进化框架实现人像修饰结果的个性化选择。将用户奖励分解为全局骨干、类别残差和轻量用户适配器三层，配合循环双级蒸馏机制，仅需极少的用户偏好排名即可校准新用户，每个新用户仅需512字节参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人像修饰的客观标准本质上是主观的：同一张肖像可以有多个专业有效的结果，不同用户对"最佳"结果的意见各不相同。现有方法大多优化群体层面的美学标准，无法捕捉个体品味差异；而为每个用户单独微调编辑模型则面临训练成本、存储开销和数据获取的巨大负担。

Method: PALATE采用共享奖励进化框架：保持图像编辑器固定，转而个性化选择同一源肖像的修饰候选。将每个用户的奖励分解为三个层级——全局共享骨干、类别级残差（服务于美学相似的用户群）和轻量用户适配器，并引入抗坍塌正则化器保持三层互补性。采用循环双级蒸馏方案：先将用户特定偏好蒸馏为类别奖励，再将类别级知识整合进全局骨干并重新分配以初始化下一轮进化。如此，共享初始化逐轮改进，使未见用户仅需少量排名即可完成校准。

Result: 在PPR10K专家修饰候选上，对留出用户和留出图像的评估中，PALATE达到72.83%的成对偏好预测准确率，超越所有奖励、美学和图像质量基线（最强基线PickScore仅58.06%）。每个新用户仅需512字节的用户特定参数和毫秒级评分成本。

Conclusion: PALATE有效解决了人像修饰中个性化偏好匹配的难题，通过共享奖励进化框架实现了以极低参数成本为用户定制修饰选择的能力，同时随着进化轮次推进，全局初始化的泛化能力持续提升，为个性化图像编辑提供了高效可行的范式。

Abstract: Automatic portrait retouching has advanced rapidly, yet its objective is inherently subjective: the same portrait admits multiple professionally valid results, and users disagree about which one is best. Most existing methods optimize a population-level aesthetic standard and therefore cannot capture individual taste, while fine-tuning a separate editing model for every user incurs prohibitive training, storage, and data costs. We propose PALATE, a shared reward-evolution framework that keeps the image editor fixed and instead personalizes the selection among retouched candidates of the same source portrait. PALATE decomposes the reward for each user into a global backbone shared by all users, category-level residuals shared by aesthetically similar users, and a lightweight user adapter, with anti-collapse regularizers keeping the three levels complementary.A cyclic dual-level distillation scheme first distills user-specific preferences into category rewards and then consolidates the resulting category-level knowledge into the global backbone, which is redistributed to initialize the next evolution round. In this way, the shared initialization improves progressively across rounds, enabling unseen users to be calibrated from only a few rankings. On expert-retouched candidates from PPR10K with held-out users and held-out images, PALATE attains 72.83% pairwise preference-prediction accuracy, surpassing all reward, aesthetic, and image-quality baselines, of which the strongest, PickScore, reaches 58.06%. Each new user costs only 512 bytes of user-specific parameters and millisecond-level scoring.

</details>

*Gašper Spagnolo,Luka Čehovin Zajc,Matej Dobrevski*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文评估了近期基于深度学习的图像匹配方法在无人机视觉里程计（VO）定位任务中的表现，使用面向下的相机和合成数据集，发现最新的RoMa匹配器效果最佳，但传统SIFT特征仍能超越部分最新方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无人机依赖GNSS导航，在信号不可用或受干扰的场景下容易发生灾难性故障。视觉里程计（VO）可作为一种关键的导航替代方案，但近期许多深度学习的图像匹配方法尚未在完整的VO系统中得到验证。

Conclusion: 深度学习方法在VO任务中有潜力但并非全面占优，传统方法（如SIFT）依然具有竞争力；RoMa是当前最有前景的匹配器选择。

Abstract: Unmanned aerial vehicles (UAVs) are becoming a powerful tool for many environmental monitoring and transport applications. Yet, their reliance on Global Navigation Satellite System (GNSS) technology for navigation makes them susceptible to catastrophic failures in scenarios where the positioning signal is unavailable or disrupted. This work explores Visual Odometry (VO) as a crucial navigation component. Recently, numerous deep-learning-based methods for image matching have been proposed that are yet to be implemented in a fully-fledged VO system. In this paper, we evaluate recent state-of-the-art image matching methods for the task of VO for UAV position tracking, with a downwards-facing camera, on our synthetic dataset, and find that while the best results are generated by the recent RoMa matcher, SIFT features can outperform some recent state-of-the-art.

</details>


### [39] [PCQA-R1: Advancing Generalized 3D Point Cloud Quality Assessment with Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18627)
*Kangning Ye,Yunhao Li,Sijing Wu,Yucheng Zhu,Guangtao Zhai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出PCQA-R1，首个基于强化学习的多模态大模型（LMM）用于三维点云质量评估。通过GRPO策略、逆向推理构建的冷启动CoT数据集以及高斯邻近奖励，实现跨数据集的高泛化能力，在五个基准上达到SOTA跨数据集泛化性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有无参考点云质量评估（PCQA）方法中，基于LMM的方法主要依赖监督微调直接预测数值质量分数，缺乏跨数据集的泛化能力，尤其在MOS尺度异构和标注有限的场景下表现不佳。绝对MOS回归在不同分数尺度和失真分布下是脆弱的，而相对质量排序则更加稳定。

Method: 1) 提出PCQA-R1，基于GRPO策略的强化学习LMM框架，同时建模质量理解与打分；2) 构建PCQA-CoT思维链数据集，通过逆向推理策略生成推理过程，作为冷启动训练数据；3) 引入高斯邻近奖励（Gaussian proximity reward），将分数预测锚定到源MOS范围，防止校准漂移。

Result: 在五个基准数据集上实现SOTA跨数据集泛化性能，同时保持具有竞争力的域内精度。消融实验验证了相对排序、高斯奖励和冷启动思维链轨迹各自的作用。

Conclusion: PCQA-R1证明了强化学习与相对排序策略相结合能够有效解决LMM在点云质量评估中的跨数据集泛化问题，为无参考PCQA提供了新的技术路径。

Abstract: No-reference point cloud quality assessment (PCQA) has been an active topic in recent years and is used to measure and optimize the visual experience of point clouds. However, large multimodal models (LMMs) have rarely been explored in this area. Previous LMM-based methods mainly rely on supervised fine-tuning to directly predict numerical quality scores, lacking the ability to generalize across datasets with heterogeneous MOS scales and limited annotations. A key difficulty is that absolute MOS regression can be brittle across datasets with different score scales and distortion distributions, whereas relative quality ranking is more stable under such shifts. In this paper, we present PCQA-R1, the first reinforcement learning LMM for 3D point cloud quality assessment to simultaneously model quality understanding and scoring. Built upon the group relative policy optimization (GRPO) strategy, PCQA-R1 first constructs a chain-of-thought dataset, PCQA-CoT, which serves as cold-start training data through a reverse reasoning strategy that teaches the LMM to generate its reasoning process. We further introduce a Gaussian proximity reward that prevents calibration drift by anchoring score predictions to the source MOS range. Experimental results demonstrate that PCQA-R1 achieves state-of-the-art cross-dataset generalization across five benchmarks and competitive in-domain accuracy. Ablation studies support the role of ranking, Gaussian reward, and cold-start traces.

</details>


### [40] [When Safety Overrides Vision: Exploring Dynamics between Vision Influence and Safety Alignment in Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18628)
*Mehak Gupta,Tanmoy Chakraborty*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究发现，在对齐的视觉语言模型（VLM）中，安全对齐虽然在解码过程中仍然保留了对视觉证据的感知接地，但会通过改变后期隐藏状态动态将生成导向拒绝行为，导致模型在安全约束指令下即使看到相同图像也会回避回答可正确回答的问题。通过激活级干预，抑制拒绝相关表征可以在不重新训练或修改视觉输入的情况下恢复基于视觉的作答。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 被对齐的视觉语言模型旨在平衡grounded视觉推理与安全生成行为。然而作者观察到在安全约束指令下，模型经常回避回答那些在默认指令下仍可正确回答的问题。这引出一个核心疑问：安全对齐究竟是抑制了感知接地本身，还是视觉证据在内部仍然可用而只是生成被导向了拒绝？这个问题之前未被充分探索。

Method: 该研究在多种架构和多种多模态基准上，调查了安全诱导弃答背后的内部解码动态。通过分析弃答生成过程中视觉证据对解码的影响，比较不同架构中拒绝行为的表征组织差异，并进行目标激活级干预实验，抑制与拒绝相关的表征以测试能否恢复接地回答行为。

Result: 研究显示：弃答生成在整个解码过程中始终受视觉证据影响，表明感知接地在很大程度上被保留；虽然拒绝的表征组织在不同架构间存在显著差异，但安全约束指令系统地改变了后期隐藏状态动态以导向拒绝解码；抑制拒绝相关表征可以可靠地恢复跨模型的接地回答行为，无需重新训练或修改视觉输入。

Conclusion: 该工作揭示了对齐VLM中一个此前未被充分探索的失败模式：安全对齐可以在感知证据在内部保持可用的情况下覆盖基于视觉的接地表达。这意味着安全对齐并非简单地破坏感知能力，而是通过改变下游解码动态来抑制视觉表达，这为理解和改进安全对齐机制提供了重要的新视角。

Abstract: Aligned vision-language models (VLMs) are designed to balance grounded visual reasoning with safe generation behavior. However, we observe a striking phenomenon: under safety-constrained instruction, models frequently abstain from answering questions that remain correctly answerable under default instruction despite receiving identical image-question inputs. This raises a fundamental question: does safety alignment suppress perceptual grounding itself, or does visual evidence remain internally available while generation is redirected toward abstention? In this work, we investigate the internal decoding dynamics underlying safety-induced abstention in aligned VLMs. Across multiple architectures and multimodal benchmarks, we show that abstained generations remain consistently influenced by visual evidence throughout decoding, indicating that perceptual grounding is largely preserved despite refusal behavior. We further demonstrate that, although the representational organization of refusal differs substantially across architectures, safety-constrained instruction consistently alters late-stage hidden-state dynamics toward refusal-oriented decoding. Finally, through targeted activation-level interventions, we show that suppressing refusal-related representations reliably restores grounded answering behavior across models without retraining or modifying visual inputs. Together, these findings reveal a previously underexplored failure mode in aligned VLMs: safety alignment can override grounded visual expression even when perceptual evidence remains internally preserved.

</details>


### [41] [SAM2Dual: Training-Free, Dual Memory for Long-Term Video Object Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18640)
*JeongRae Kim,Changwon Lim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SAM2Dual是一种无需训练、即插即用的推理时增强方法，通过双记忆（短期+长期）设计和文本感知记忆机制，在不更新模型权重的情况下提升SAM2在长视频目标分割中的鲁棒性，有效缓解长时漂移和遮挡下的误差累积问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长视频目标分割（VOS）在长时间遮挡、重现和场景变化下仍面临挑战。SAM2虽具备强大零样本性能，但其流式记忆机制在长时程中易被近期不可靠预测主导，导致漂移放大。作者旨在解决长视频中误差累积和身份保持的问题，且不依赖模型权重更新。

Method: SAM2Dual包含两个核心组件：(1) 双记忆设计（Dual Memory），将短期记忆（快速局部自适应）与通过间隔采样构建的长期记忆（保持全局身份线索）分离，并通过门控融合策略结合二者；(2) 文本感知记忆（TAM），从早期帧提取紧凑的词级线索，利用文本嵌入根据语义兼容性对记忆贡献进行重加权，在视觉证据弱或模糊时支持身份保持。整体为训练免费的推理时增强方案。

Result: 在长视频基准上，SAM2Dual一致提升了长视频稳定性：在MOSEv2上将J&F从49.33提升至50.65，在LVOSv2上也取得了一致的性能提升。

Conclusion: SAM2Dual通过显式分离长短期记忆并引入文本语义线索，有效缓解了长视频中的漂移和身份丢失问题，是一种通用、无需训练的长视频分割增强方案，具备即插即用的实用性。

Abstract: Long-term video object segmentation (VOS) remains challenging due to error accumulation under extended occlusions, re-appearance, and scene changes. Although SAM2 provides strong zero-shot performance, its streaming memory can amplify drift over long horizons when recent, unreliable predictions dominate the memory state. We propose SAM2Dual, a training-free, plug-and-play inference-time enhancement that improves long-video robustness without updating model weights. SAM2Dual introduces a Dual Memory design that explicitly separates (i) short-term memory for rapid local adaptation and (ii) long-term memory built via interval-based sampling to preserve global identity cues, combined through a gated fusion strategy. In addition, we present Text-Aware Memory (TAM), which extracts a compact word-level cue from early frames and uses text embeddings to reweight memory contributions based on semantic compatibility, supporting identity preservation when visual evidence becomes weak or ambiguous. Across long-term benchmarks, SAM2Dual consistently improves stability on long videos, raising J&F from 49.33 to 50.65 on MOSEv2 and achieving consistent gains on LVOSv2.

</details>


### [42] [Clinically Structured Surrogate Rewards for Post-SFT Medical Image Captioning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18654)
*Hyun Jun Kim,Heeseung Shin,Changwon Lim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种临床结构化的替代奖励框架用于医学图像字幕生成的后SFT优化，结合生物医学语义与短程词汇保真度，以及两种结构化奖励（图像邻域分布对齐和临床图一致性），通过GDPO优化，在ImageCLEFmedical Caption任务上平均相对提升Overall 3.4%、Relevance 2.1%、Factuality 5.8%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有医学图像字幕生成虽然表面流畅，但可能在发现、断言状态或解剖关系上出错而改变临床含义。序列级策略优化能直接优化完整字幕，但常见奖励依赖全局文本相似度、直接图像-字幕兼容性或无序概念重叠，忽视了视觉邻域和临床声明结构。

Method: 提出临床结构化替代奖励框架，包含四个奖励：生物医学语义匹配+短程词汇保真度，以及两个结构化奖励——（1）分布性图像邻域对齐（匹配参考和生成字幕在医学图像库上诱导的分布），（2）临床图一致性（对实体、断言状态和类型化关系应用最大权重一对一匹配）。四个奖励在每组rollout内独立归一化，以固定相对权重组合，并用GDPO（Generalized Direct Preference Optimization）优化。

Result: 在ImageCLEFmedical Caption标准与合成赛道、三种视觉语言骨干上，全部六个骨干-赛道组合均优于匹配的SFT基线，平均相对提升Overall 3.4%、Relevance 2.1%、Factuality 5.8%。消融与配对诊断表明结构化奖励提供互补信号，减少图像邻域发散并改善实体-断言-关系一致性。

Conclusion: 结构化奖励与通用的语义/词汇奖励互补，能有效改善医学图像字幕的临床准确性与事实性，验证了结合临床语义结构和视觉邻域分布进行策略优化的有效性。

Abstract: Medical image captioning requires translating heterogeneous visual evidence into concise clinical descriptions, where errors in findings, assertion states, or anatomical relations can alter clinical meaning despite surface-level fluency. Sequence-level policy optimization can directly optimize complete captions, but common rewards rely on global text similarity, direct image-caption compatibility, or unordered concept overlap, leaving visual neighborhoods and clinical-claim structure implicit. We propose a clinically structured surrogate reward framework for post-SFT medical image captioning. The framework combines biomedical semantic and short-range lexical fidelity with two structured rewards: distributional image-neighborhood alignment, which matches the medical-image-bank distributions induced by reference and generated captions, and clinical graph consistency, which applies maximum-weight one-to-one matching to entities, assertion states, and typed relations. The four rewards are independently normalized within each rollout group, combined with fixed relative weights, and optimized with GDPO. Across organizer-evaluated hidden test sets for the Standard and Synthetical ImageCLEFmedical Caption tracks and three vision-language backbones, the method improves Overall, Relevance, and Factuality over matched SFT baselines in all six backbone-track combinations, with average relative gains of 3.4%, 2.1%, and 5.8%, respectively. Ablations and paired diagnostics indicate that the structured rewards provide complementary signals, reducing image-neighborhood divergence and improving entity-assertion-relation consistency.

</details>


### [43] [Dynamic SpectraFormer for Ultra-High-Definition Underwater Image Enhancement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18662)
*Zhiqiang Hu,Tao Yu,Shouren Huang,Masatoshi Ishikawa*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种名为Dynamic SpectraFormer的频率域Transformer方法，通过超高清稀疏频谱注意力模块和动态频谱权重生成层，同时校正水下图像中的低频（颜色和亮度）和高频（边缘和纹理）失真，在多个水下图像增强基准上验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 水下图像因光在水中的折射和吸收而遭受颜色失真、雾化和低可见度问题，这些挑战严重影响了AUV和海洋机器人的应用。传统方法主要聚焦于空间域处理，难以同时修正这些混合失真，因为颜色和亮度失真主要存在于低频，而边缘和纹理失真存在于高频。

Method: 提出的Dynamic SpectraFormer是一个频率域Transformer，包含两个关键组件：(1) 超高清稀疏频谱注意力模块，能够在保持通用逼近能力的同时捕获长程依赖；(2) 动态频谱权重生成层，作为自适应频谱波段选择器，突出关键频率波段并抑制不相关波段。

Result: 通过广泛的消融研究和对比评估，Dynamic SpectraFormer在多个水下图像增强基准上展现了良好的性能，证明该方法能有效同时处理高、低频失真，显著提升水下图像质量。

Conclusion: Dynamic SpectraFormer通过频率域Transformer架构同时解决水下图像的高频和低频失真问题，为水下图像增强提供了新的有效方案，源码已公开供进一步研究。

Abstract: Underwater images suffer from color distortion, haze, and poor visibility due to light refraction and absorption in water. These challenges significantly impact the utilization of Autonomous Underwater Vehicles (AUVs) or marine robots. Typically, color and brightness distortions manifest at lower frequencies, while edge and texture distortions are prevalent at higher frequencies. Traditional methods struggle to concurrently rectify these mixed distortions as they primarily concentrate on the spatial domain. To address these issues, we introduce the Dynamic SpectraFormer, which enhances underwater images through a frequency domain transformer. The Dynamic SpectraFormer introduces an ultra-high-resolution sparse spectrum attention module, which could capture the long-term dependency without losing the universal approximating power. Additionally, we have developed a dynamic spectrum weight generation layer that serves as an adaptive spectrum band selector, accentuating critical frequency bands and suppressing less relevant ones. Consequently, this method significantly improves underwater image quality by addressing both high- and low-frequency distortions. Our extensive ablation studies and comparative evaluations consolidate the Dynamic SpectraFormer's efficacy across multiple underwater image enhancement benchmarks. The source code is available at https://github.com/arifence2024/DynamicSpectraFormer.git.

</details>


### [44] [Teeth2Point: A Two-Stage Dental CBCT ROI-to-Point Segmentation Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18667)
*Qi Ma,Shipra Jain,Niko Benjamin Huber,Ender Konukoglu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Teeth2Point，一种高效的基于点云Transformer的牙科CBCT语义分割框架。该方法通过卷积模型定位牙齿感兴趣区域（ROI），再利用自适应采样将ROI转换为点token，使Transformer能在保持高分辨率的同时捕获全局上下文，避免了密集patch处理中的计算折衷。模型先采用DINO风格的自监督预训练（含随机token掩码和领域特定数据增强），再监督微调。在四个数据集上，异常病例分割性能较最强两阶段基线平均提升1.44个DSC点，较第一阶nnU-Net提升1.9个点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 牙科CBCT分割在牙齿缺失或错位（异常病例）场景下仍面临精确牙齿标注的挑战。虽然Transformer理论上可利用全局解剖上下文解决此类歧义，但CBCT体数据高分辨率与牙齿空间分布广泛导致密集patch处理存在固有权衡：计算成本限制了自注意力可用的patch数量，使得在扩大上下文范围和细粒度结构细节之间只能二选一。因此需要一种能同时兼顾全局上下文与高分辨率的方法。

Method: Teeth2Point框架分为两个阶段：（1）使用卷积模型定位包含牙齿的体素ROI；（2）将ROI通过自适应采样转换为点token输入Transformer进行分割预测。Transformer采用DINO风格的SSL预训练，结合领域特定的数据增强策略和随机token掩码，随后进行有监督微调。点token表示使模型能够在保持高分辨率细节的同时捕获全局解剖上下文，从而规避密集patch处理中的计算折衷问题。

Result: 在四个数据集上的实验表明，Teeth2Point相比最强的两阶段基线方法，在异常病例上的平均分割性能提升了1.44个DSC点；相比第一阶段的nnU-Net，性能提升1.9个DSC点。

Conclusion: Teeth2Point通过点token化的Transformer框架有效解决了密集patch处理中全局上下文与局部细节不可兼得的矛盾，显著提升了牙齿缺失/错位等异常病例的分割精度，验证了SSL预训练与领域自适应增强策略在此任务中的有效性。

Abstract: Modern deep learning architectures have demonstrated strong performance in dental CBCT segmentation. One remaining crucial challenge is accurate tooth labeling in cases with missing or malpositioned teeth, which are highly relevant for dental practice. Transformer-based architectures should in theory be able to resolve such ambiguities using global anatomical context. However, due to the high resolution of CBCT volumes and the wide spatial distribution of teeth within volumes, dense patch-based volumetric processing faces an inherent trade-off. Computational costs limit the number of patches that can be used in self-attention and thus, one can either increase the extent of the context captured in self-attention or capture fine-grained structural details by using small patches, but not both. In this work, we present Teeth2Point, an efficient point-based transformer framework for dental CBCT semantic segmentation that can avoid this trade-off. Teeth2Point first localizes volumetric regions of interest (ROIs) surrounding teeth using a convolutional model, then converts ROIs into point tokens using adaptive sampling. A transformer model predicts accurate segmentations using the point tokens, which allow capturing global context while retaining high resolution. The transformer is first pretrained using self-supervised learning (SSL), in the style of DINO but using domain-specific augmentation strategies, followed by supervised finetuning. The SSL pretraining, which includes random token masking, provides robustness to complex anatomical variations. Compared with the strongest two-stage baseline, Teeth2Point improves abnormal-case performance by 1.44 DSC points on average across four datasets; relative to the first-stage nnU-Net, the gain is 1.9 points.

</details>


### [45] [Vision-Language Models for Egocentric Video: From Hand-Object Interaction to Embodied AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18671)
*Mohammad Zamani,Fatemeh Ziaeetabar*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文系统综述了用于自我中心视频理解的视觉-语言模型（VLMs），梳理了从传统识别架构到多模态基础模型和具身系统的演进过程，并指出了当前模型在动态交互、动作和用户意图理解上的局限性以及未来优先研究方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自我中心视频在可穿戴智能、辅助系统、人机交互和具身AI中日益重要，但其面临自我运动、遮挡、小目标、视角依赖外观和长时依赖等挑战。视觉-语言模型为应对这些挑战提供了有前景的基础，因此有必要对该领域进行全面梳理，明确进展、局限与未来方向。

Method: 以批判性综述的方式组织文献，围绕任务、数据集、手-物交互理解、时序推理、帧与片段选择、多模态表示学习、提示、语义对齐和模型适配等维度展开讨论，特别关注基于图和以对象为中心的推理机制，用于建模手、对象、动作与场景上下文之间的时序关系。

Result: 综述发现当前模型对可见对象的识别比对演化中的交互、动作和用户意图更可靠，尤其在长时活动中表现明显。识别出的关键研究优先方向包括：时序接地推理、交互感知监督、高效长视频处理、多模态融合、图增强表示、跨域泛化、隐私保护和可信评估。

Conclusion: 自我中心视频理解正从识别范式走向多模态基础模型与具身系统，未来需要重点解决时序推理、交互感知的监督信号和高效长视频处理等核心问题，才能实现具备可用性的具身智能部署。

Abstract: Egocentric video captures activities from the wearer's perspective, providing a direct view of human attention, hand--object interaction, and goal-directed behavior. This perspective is increasingly important for wearable intelligence, assistive systems, human--robot interaction, and embodied AI, yet it introduces challenges including ego-motion, occlusion, small active objects, viewpoint-dependent appearance, and long-range temporal dependencies. Vision--language models (VLMs) offer a promising foundation for addressing these challenges by linking visual observations with semantic knowledge and natural-language supervision. This survey presents a critical review of VLMs for egocentric video understanding, tracing the progression from conventional recognition architectures to multimodal foundation models and embodied systems. We organize the literature around tasks, datasets, hand--object interaction understanding, temporal reasoning, frame and clip selection, multimodal representation learning, prompting, semantic alignment, and model adaptation. Particular attention is given to graph-based and object-centric reasoning as mechanisms for modeling relations among hands, objects, actions, and scene context over time. We further examine how first-person perception and multimodal foundation models support wearable assistance, robot skill learning, human-to-robot transfer, and embodied decision making. Across the reviewed literature, a consistent limitation emerges: current models recognize visible objects more reliably than evolving interactions, actions, and user intent, especially over long activities. We therefore identify temporally grounded reasoning, interaction-aware supervision, efficient long-video processing, multimodal fusion, graph-enhanced representations, cross-domain generalization, privacy, and trustworthy evaluation as key priorities for deployable embodied intelligence.

</details>


### [46] [DynCur-Geo: Dynamic Curiosity Reward Shaping for Multimodal Active Geo-Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18673)
*Yiming Sun,Yang Zhang,Pengfei Zhu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出DynCur-Geo，一种动态好奇框架，通过距离感知门控调整预测误差内在奖励权重，实现从探索到目标收敛的策略平滑过渡，提升低空无人机主动地理定位性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低空无人机主动地理定位面临多模态目标线索、受限视野和稀疏反馈的挑战，需要在探索与目标收敛之间取得平衡。现有基于好奇心的强化学习方法使用固定内在奖励权重，在智能体接近目标时仍持续奖励新异性，导致绕路行为，降低了搜索效率。

Method: DynCur-Geo采用动态好奇框架：(1) 距离感知门控根据剩余目标距离动态调整预测误差内在奖励的权重，早期鼓励广泛探索，接近目标时强化目标导向策略；(2) 基于势能的奖励塑形提供稠密进度引导，辅助稀疏反馈下的学习。

Result: 在多模态、跨场景、灾后受损和长距离等多种实验设置下，DynCur-Geo在主动地理定位基线上持续取得性能提升。

Conclusion: 动态调整好奇内在奖励比重能有效解决探索与利用的矛盾，提升无人机主动地理定位的搜索效率和目标收敛能力，验证了距离感知奖励设计在时序敏感任务中的有效性。

Abstract: Active geo-localization enables low-altitude UAVs to search for specified targets from limited local aerial observations, supporting time-sensitive applications such as search and rescue and emergency inspection. However, multimodal target cues, restricted views, and sparse feedback make it difficult to balance exploration with target convergence. Existing curiosity-driven methods assign a fixed intrinsic-reward weight throughout search, which can continue rewarding novelty after the agent nears the target and induce detours. We propose DynCur-Geo, a dynamic curiosity framework that adjusts prediction-error intrinsic reward according to remaining target distance. A distance-aware gate encourages early exploration and shifts the policy toward goal-directed behavior near the target, while potential-based reward shaping supplies dense progress guidance. Experiments across multimodal, cross-scene, disaster-affected, and long-range settings show consistent gains over active geo-localization baselines.

</details>


### [47] [FRAGMENT: Factorized Graph Representations for Document Generation and Editing via Entity-Aware Transformations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18679)
*Ayoub El Bouchtili,Guilhaume Leroy-Meline*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FRAGMENT是一个两阶段的生成式文档建模框架，将文档表示为类型化关系图，通过分解p(structure)和p(content|structure)来捕捉文档的空间布局、文本内容和逻辑结构之间的依赖关系，并支持伪造检测和受控编辑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 结构化文档（如发票、表单、报告和科学文章）的语义来源于空间布局、文本内容和逻辑结构之间的交互，而现有的像素级或token级生成模型难以有效捕捉这些依赖关系，因此需要一种能够显式建模结构-内容依赖的生成框架。

Method: FRAGMENT将文档建模为类型化关系图，将分布分解为p(structure)*p(content|structure)。第一阶段Architect使用条件于文档类别的因果掩码Transformer自回归生成图拓扑和类型化空间关系；第二阶段Builder使用基于GATv2的图注意力网络丰富图的归一化边界框、文本和视觉风格属性。两个阶段均定义显式似然模型，提供可计算的文档级似然用于伪造检测。还通过交叉注意力注入指令嵌入实现提示条件的受控编辑。

Result: 在DocLayNet上进行训练，并在FUNSD和SROIE上微调。实验在三个数据集上与代表性的自回归、仅布局和图基线方法进行对比，提供了对因子化图生成框架特性和权衡的实证分析。

Conclusion: FRAGMENT通过将文档生成分解为结构生成和内容生成两个阶段，提供了可处理的文档级似然，在生成质量、伪造检测（异常分数）和受控编辑方面展现出优势，为结构化文档建模提供了新的方向。

Abstract: Structured documents such as invoices, forms, reports, and scientific articles derive meaning from the interplay between spatial layout, textual content, and logical structure. Generative models operating at the pixel or token level often struggle to capture these dependencies effectively. We explore FRAGMENT, a generative framework that represents a document as a typed relational graph and factorizes its distribution as p(structure, content) = p(structure) * p(content | structure). The framework consists of two stages. The first stage, the Architect, is a causally masked Transformer conditioned on document category that autoregressively generates the graph topology and typed spatial relations. The second stage, the Builder, is a GATv2-based graph attention network that enriches the graph with normalized bounding boxes, text, and visual style attributes. Both stages define explicit likelihood models, yielding a tractable document-level likelihood that serves as an anomaly score for forgery detection. For controlled editing, a prompt-conditioned extension injects instruction embeddings into the Builder through cross-attention, enabling semantic and entity-aware modifications. We describe training on DocLayNet and fine-tuning on FUNSD and SROIE. Experiments on DocLayNet, FUNSD, and SROIE evaluate FRAGMENT alongside representative autoregressive, layout-only, and graph-based baselines, providing an empirical analysis of the characteristics and trade-offs of the proposed factorized graph generation framework.

</details>


### [48] [DocClaw: A Unified Agentic System for Intelligent Document Processing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18685)
*Siqi Xiang,Zhipeng Xu,Yufei Liu,Junhao Ji,Qing Liu,Zulong Chen,Zhibo Yang,Chunyan Miao,Shijian Lu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DocClaw是一个统一的智能体系统，将OCR、文档问答和关键信息抽取等智能文档处理任务统一表述为智能体与文档之间的迭代交互过程，通过结构化的文档状态组织可复用知识，在单一框架下实现了与通用视觉语言模型和任务专用方法相当的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能文档处理中的各类任务（OCR、DocQA、KIE）虽然在目标上各异，但本质上都共享感知文档内容、获取任务相关信息并逐步精化中间结果的过程。然而，现有方法通常将不同任务视为独立的预测问题，采用任务特定模型或处理流水线，缺乏统一框架。

Method: DocClaw将各类文档处理任务统一表述为智能体与文档之间的交互过程。具体而言：(1) 给定文档和任务查询，智能体依据相应的文档技能（document skill）迭代识别所需信息、调用相关工具、并将观测结果整合为期望输出；(2) 整个过程中，一个结构化的文档状态（document state）组织可复用的文档知识和任务特定的交互上下文，使智能体能够累积、回溯并逐步精化信息；(3) 任务特定需求由智能体对查询目标和对应文档技能的解释来捕获，而底层的交互循环、工具空间和文档状态在所有任务间共享。

Result: 在多个智能文档处理基准上的广泛实验表明，DocClaw能够在单一智能体框架内有效处理多种任务，并且达到了与通用视觉语言模型和任务专用方法相竞争的性能水平。

Conclusion: DocClaw通过统一的智能体交互范式，验证了多样化的智能文档处理任务可以被统一在单一框架下解决，且无需牺牲性能，为智能文档处理提供了一个可扩展的统一架构方向。

Abstract: Intelligent document processing (IDP) encompasses a broad range of tasks, including optical character recognition (OCR), document question answering (DocQA), and key information extraction (KIE). Despite their distinct objectives, these tasks share a common need to perceive document content, acquire task-relevant information, and progressively refine intermediate results. However, they are typically formulated as separate prediction problems and addressed by task-specific models or processing pipelines. We introduce DocClaw, a unified agentic system that formulates diverse intelligent document processing tasks as a shared process of interaction between an agent and a document. Given a document and a task-specific query, DocClaw follows an appropriate document skill to iteratively identify the information required, invoke relevant tools, and integrate the resulting observations into the desired output. Throughout this process, a structured document state organizes reusable document knowledge and task-specific interaction context, allowing the agent to accumulate, revisit, and progressively refine information as the interaction proceeds. Under this formulation, task-specific requirements are captured by the agent's interpretation of the query objective and the corresponding document skill, while the underlying interaction loop, tool space, and document state are shared across tasks. Extensive experiments across multiple intelligent document processing benchmarks demonstrate that DocClaw effectively handles diverse tasks within a single agentic framework and achieves competitive performance compared with both general-purpose VLMs and task-specific methods.

</details>


### [49] [Composed Historical Image Retrieval by Modeling Temporal Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18694)
*Adrià Molina Rodríguez,Oriol Ramos Terrades,Josep Lladós Canet*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出TDIR（时间可分解图像表示）算法，将历史照片的嵌入空间分解为正交的时间子空间和内容子空间，使得时间信息可以在不同图像之间传递，无需标签监督即可实现组合图像检索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经嵌入空间是多元且难以解释的，缺乏对时间结构的显式建模。虽然可以将嵌入限制为单一时间维度，但这会牺牲下游任务的表达能力。作者希望找到一种既能保留时间结构又不会损害图像/物体检索性能的表示学习方法。

Method: 提出TDIR算法，通过正交子空间将历史照片的嵌入分解为独立的日期成分和内容成分。作者定义了这种分解可实现的条件并给出证明，刻画了条件部分满足时的误差界，并证明正交性可以从联合优化中自然涌现而无需显式约束。此外，TDIR支持嵌入空间的传递操作：将一张图像的时间信息提取并注入到另一张图像的表征中。

Result: 所有理论性质均在历史照片的组合图像检索任务中得到验证，在日期估计和物体检索上均保持有竞争力的性能，同时提供了直观可解释的方式来浏览照片档案。

Conclusion: TDIR在理论和实践上都验证了将时间结构与内容结构正交分解的可行性，为可解释的嵌入空间导航提供了新范式，且无需标签监督即可实现时间信息的跨图像传递。

Abstract: While time evolves linearly, the geometry of neural embedding spaces is inherently multi-dimensional, often chaotic, and difficult to interpret. In principle, one could constrain an embedding space to a single temporal dimension; however, such a reduction would sacrifice performance on downstream tasks, as one-dimensional embeddings cannot retain sufficient expressive capacity. This paper asks whether it is possible to learn representations that preserve temporal structure while remaining effective for image and object retrieval, and answers this question by building the mathematical foundations of such a system. We propose Temporally Decomposable Image Representations (TDIR), a representation learning algorithm that decomposes historical photographs into separate date and content components through orthogonal subspaces. We define and prove the conditions under which such a decomposition is achievable, characterize the error incurred when those conditions are only partially met, and show that orthogonality between temporal and categorical subspaces emerges naturally from the joint optimization, without requiring it to be imposed explicitly. Beyond its geometric properties, TDIR enables a class of transitive operations on embedding spaces: the temporal information of one image can be extracted and injected into the representation of another, with no label supervision required. All theoretical properties are grounded and validated in the real-world problem of Composed Image Retrieval on historical photographs, where a query simultaneously specifies object content and a target time period, either through labels or through example images. This in-the-wild setting serves as a concrete backing for the propositions we derive, offering an intuitive and interpretable way to navigate photographic archives while maintaining competitive performance in both date estimation and object retrieval.

</details>


### [50] [Impact of Iterative Fine-Tuning on Transcription Accuracy in Complex Historical Sanskrit Manuscripts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18696)
*Kartik Chincholikar,Kaushik Gopalan,Mihir Hasabnis*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种可迭代微调的传统OCR流水线，用于数字化复杂历史手稿（特别是梵语手稿），并在布局层面和外观层面适应该目标手稿的分布，从而减少人工标注成本。作者还引入了带有细粒度布局标注和Unicode标注的PAGE-XML格式数据集，并基准测试了多模态大语言模型的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 历史手稿常常具有复杂的异构布局和非标准外观（如特定时期的书写风格、页面纹理、相机噪声等），使得传统OCR难以处理。而人工标注需要历史领域专业知识，成本高昂且耗时，因此需要一种能够通过迭代微调逐步适应用户手稿、减少人工干预的OCR方案。

Method: 提出一个本地传统OCR流水线，能够在布局层面（layout-level）和外观层面（appearance-level）对目标手稿进行迭代微调。该流水线通过适配目标手稿的数据分布，在后续页面上做出更好的预测，从而在迭代过程中不断降低人工标注负担。作者将流水线应用于三个复杂的梵语历史手稿，并构建了带有细粒度布局标注和Unicode标注的PAGE-XML格式数据集。

Result: 实验表明，该传统OCR流水线的迭代微调带来了量化性能增益。同时，作者在引入的数据集上基准测试了领先的多模态大语言模型（Multi-Modal LLMs）的性能，并与所提OCR流水线进行了对比。

Conclusion: 所提出的可迭代微调传统OCR流水线能有效适配目标历史手稿的布局和外观分布，显著提升识别精度并降低人工标注成本。该工作不仅提供了实际可用的OCR解决方案，还贡献了带有详细标注的历史手稿数据集，为后续研究提供了基准。

Abstract: Digitizing the text from handwritten historical manuscripts is required to make them easily accessible, preservable, and to enable historical scholars to study them in new ways. Historical manuscripts, however, often exhibit complex heterogeneous layouts and non-standard appearance due to period-specific writing styles, page textures, camera noise, and other nuisance factors, making them difficult to perform OCR on. To tackle this challenge, we introduce a local traditional OCR pipeline, which can be iteratively fine-tuned on the target manuscript at the layout-level and the appearance-level. By adapting to the target manuscript distribution, the proposed Traditional OCR pipeline makes better predictions on subsequent pages, causing iterative reduction in human annotation effort, which is expensive and time-consuming as it requires historical domain expertise. Using this pipeline, we digitize text from three complex historical Sanskrit manuscripts and introduce a dataset with granular layout-level annotations, along with Unicode annotations in the standard PAGE-XML format. We demonstrate quantitative gains due to iterative fine-tuning of the proposed traditional OCR pipeline, and also benchmark the performance of leading Multi-Modal Large Language Models on the introduced Dataset. Code and dataset are available at: https://github.com/flame-cai/gnn-synthetic-layout-historical/.

</details>


### [51] [A Critical Synthesis of Uncertainty Quantification and Foundation Models for Semantic Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18709)
*Steven Landgraf,Joceline Hinz,Markus Ulrich*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 首次系统评估了不确定性量化（UQ）方法在语义分割基础模型（SAM2编码器+DPT解码器）中的应用，基准测试了四种UQ方法在多个数据集和域外场景下的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基础模型在精度和跨域泛化上表现优异，但缺乏可解释性、过度自信且对真实世界域偏移敏感，威胁安全关键应用。UQ是解决这些问题的原则性手段，但尚未在分割基础模型中得到探索。

Method: 在预训练SAM2编码器之上微调轻量级DPT解码器构建基线，并基准测试四种代表性UQ方法：蒙特卡洛Dropout、深度子集成、测试时增强和证据深度学习，在Cityscapes、NYUv2及两个挑战性域外设置下进行比较。

Result: 比较了分割精度、校准质量、不确定性质量和推理时间，揭示了预测性能、可靠性与计算成本之间的明显权衡。

Conclusion: 不确定性感知基础模型具有前景但仍存在局限性，未来需要联合优化精度、鲁棒性和效率以支持实际部署。

Abstract: Foundation models are increasingly breaking what seemed to be impossible not long ago by enabling unprecedented accuracy and cross-domain generalization. Yet their lack of interpretability, tendency to be overconfident, and sensitivity to real-world domain shifts pose critical challenges for safety- and mission-critical applications. Uncertainty quantification (UQ) offers a principled way to address these issues, but its integration into segmentation foundation models has yet to be explored. In this paper we present the first systematic evaluation of UQ methods applied to a foundation model for semantic segmentation. We fine-tune a lightweight DPT decoder on top of the pretrained SAM2 encoder to establish a simple yet competitive baseline and benchmark four representative UQ approaches - Monte Carlo Dropout, Deep Sub-Ensemble, Test-Time Augmentation, and Evidential Deep Learning - across Cityscapes, NYUv2, and two challenging out-of-domain settings. Our analysis compares segmentation accuracy, calibration, uncertainty quality, and inference time, revealing clear trade-offs between predictive performance, reliability, and computational cost. These results highlight both the promise and the current limitations of uncertainty-aware foundation models, pointing to the need for future work that jointly optimizes accuracy, robustness, and efficiency for real-world deployment.

</details>


### [52] [CamWorldQA: Perceptual Quality Assessment of Camera-Controlled World Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18710)
*Yunhe Li,Likun Wu,Sijing Wu,Xinyu Tian,Huiyu Duan,Yixuan Gao,Yunhao Li,Guangtao Zhai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了CamWorldQA，这是第一个面向相机控制世界视频生成的感知质量评估基准，包含720个由6种生成方法、20个源视频和6种相机轨迹生成的视频及人类评分的质量标注；同时提出了CWQA，一个具有空间、时序运动、光流三个互补分支的无参考质量评估网络。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频质量评估(VQA)方法主要针对自然视频设计，无法捕捉相机控制生成视频的独特感知特征（如视角一致性、运动连贯性和内容保持），因此需要专门针对这一新兴任务的质量评估基准和方法。

Method: 构建了包含720个生成视频的CamWorldQA基准数据集，涵盖6种生成方法、20个源视频和6种相机轨迹，并通过主观实验获取人类评分。在此基础上提出CWQA网络，采用三个互补分支分别提取空间特征、时序运动特征和光流特征，联合预测质量分数。

Result: 在CamWorldQA数据集上的大量实验表明，CWQA的性能优于现有视频质量评估方法。

Conclusion: CWQA在相机控制世界视频生成的感知质量评估任务上取得了优于现有方法的表现，验证了多分支特征融合架构在该特定场景下的有效性。

Abstract: Recent advances in generative video models have enabled camera-controlled world video generation, allowing models to synthesize videos under user-defined camera trajectories. However, existing video quality assessment (VQA) methods are mainly developed for natural videos and fail to capture the unique perceptual characteristics of camera-controlled generation, such as viewpoint consistency, motion coherence, and content preservation. In this work, we introduce CamWorldQA, the first benchmark for perceptual quality assessment of camera-controlled world video generation. CamWorldQA contains 720 generated videos produced by 6 representative generation methods from 20 diverse source videos under 6 camera trajectories, where each video is annotated with a human-rated perceptual quality score through subjective experiments. Furthermore, we propose CWQA, a no-reference quality assessment network with three complementary branches that extract spatial features, temporal motion features and optical flow features to jointly predict quality scores. Extensive experiments demonstrate that CWQA achieves superior performance over existing quality assessment methods on the CamWorldQA dataset.

</details>


### [53] [EgoHRV: Continuous Heart Rate Variability Estimation from Egocentric Systems for Autonomic Response and Skill Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18711)
*Berken Utku Demirel,Christian Holz*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: EgoHRV是一种从第一人称（自我中心）视觉系统中估计心率变异性（HRV）和心率（HR）的方法，利用头盔已有的注视摄像头提取血容量脉冲（BVP）信号。通过结合3D主干网络与低-高分解模块，并在跨域预训练中对齐接触式与摄像头信号的频域表示，EgoHRV实现了从注视视频中细微波动恢复HRV的时序精度，在HR与HRV估计上达到最先进水平，并将EgoExo4D熟练度估计准确率提升17.8%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的自我中心视觉系统仅从可见线索捕捉人类行为，忽略了如压力、投入度和注意力等自主神经状态的生理指标。HRV作为广泛使用的非侵入性自主神经调节标记物，反映了心跳之间细微的时间差异，但此前受限于运动与注视视频噪声，无法在自我中心平台上精确估计。

Method: 提出EgoHRV管线，包含：(1) 3D骨干网络提取时空特征；(2) 新颖的低-高分解模块从注视视频中提取BVP信号；(3) 跨域预训练将对齐接触式传感器与摄像头信号的频域表征，赋予模型在注视视频细微波动中恢复HRV所需的时序精度；(4) 不确定性感知设计以支持下游行为建模。

Result: EgoHRV在从自我中心视频估计HR和HRV方面达到最先进准确率。将HRV估计及其置信度整合进EgoExo4D的熟练度估计器后，准确率提升17.8%。

Conclusion: EgoHRV证实了从自我中心注视摄像头估计连续HRV的可行性，使自我中心系统除了技能评估外，还能扩展至压力感知与唤醒状态感知等估计任务，拓宽了可穿戴智能头显在自主神经状态监测方面的应用范围。

Abstract: Egocentric vision systems capture human behavior from visible cues, but overlook physiological indicators of autonomic states such as stress, engagement, and attention. Heart rate variability (HRV) is a widely used noninvasive marker of autonomic regulation under stress. HRV reflects small timing differences between successive heartbeats and has so far been out of reach for egocentric platforms, where motion and noise in gaze video mask exactly this fine-grained timing. We propose EgoHRV, a method that estimates HRV as well as heart rate (HR) from the gaze cameras that are already integrated into egocentric headsets. Our pipeline combines a 3D backbone with a novel low--high decomposition module that extracts the blood volume pulse (BVP) signal from gaze video. Our cross-domain pretraining aligns the frequency-domain representations of contact-based and camera-derived signals. This alignment gives EgoHRV the temporal precision to recover HRV from the subtle fluctuations in gaze video. EgoHRV achieves state-of-the-art accuracy for HR and HRV estimation from egocentric video, and its uncertainty-aware design improves downstream behavioral modeling. Integrating our HRV estimates and confidence measures into EgoExo4D's proficiency estimator raises accuracy by 17.8%. Beyond skill, continuous HRV estimation also opens egocentric systems to stress- and arousal-aware estimation tasks. Code: https://github.com/eth-siplab/EgoHRV

</details>


### [54] [The Impact of CutMix on Reliability and Robustness in Semantic Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18715)
*Steven Landgraf,Markus Ulrich*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本论文系统研究了CutMix数据增强策略对语义分割模型可靠性和鲁棒性的影响，发现CutMix对分割精度影响甚微，但能一致性地改善模型在校准和不确定性估计方面的可靠性，尤其是在分布偏移场景下。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语义分割模型在自动驾驶等安全关键应用中的部署不仅需要高精度，还需要可靠且稳健的预测。尽管CutMix被广泛使用，但它在密集预测任务中对模型可靠性和鲁棒性的影响尚未被探索。此外，近期研究发现以CutMix为核心组件的半监督分割方法会严重降低可靠性，这促使研究者系统性地隔离并分析CutMix的作用。

Method: 该研究系统性地隔离并分析了CutMix对分割精度、校准和不确定性质量三方面的影响。实验评估了两种代表性架构：基于CNN的DeepLabV3+和基于Transformer的SegFormer，覆盖域内和域外两种场景。

Result: 实验结果表明：CutMix对分割精度影响较小，但一致性改善了模型可靠性，尤其在分布偏移条件下改善更为明显。这意味着CutMix主要提升的是模型校准和不确定性估计的可信度，而非原始分割预测本身。

Conclusion: CutMix对可靠性的提升与其对精度的有限影响之间的区分对于安全关键部署至关重要，因为在这些场景中，可靠的置信度估计与原始性能同等重要。

Abstract: Ensuring not only high accuracy but also reliable and robust predictions is critical for the deployment of semantic segmentation models in safety-critical applications such as autonomous driving. Despite the widespread use of CutMix - a simple yet powerful data augmentation strategy - its effect on the reliability and robustness in dense predictions tasks remains unexplored. Motivated by recent findings that semi-supervised segmentation methods, where CutMix is a core component, can severely degrade reliability, this study isolates and systematically analyzes the influence of CutMix on segmentation accuracy, calibration, and uncertainty quality. We evaluate two representative architectures, the CNN-based DeepLabV3+ and the transformer-based SegFormer, across both in-domain and out-of-domain scenarios. Our results show that CutMix has only a minor impact on segmentation accuracy but consistently improves the reliability, particularly under distribution shifts. These improvements indicate that CutMix primarily enhances the trustworthiness of the model's calibration and uncertainty rather than the raw segmentation prediction itself. This distinction is crucial for safety-critical deployment, where reliable confidence estimates are as important as raw performance.

</details>


### [55] [A Few Cases Are All You Need: An Empirical Study of Annotation-Efficient LoRA Fine-Tuning of MedSAM3](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18731)
*Sachin Dudda Nagaraju,Bendik Skarre Abrahamsen,Ashkan Moradi,Mattijs Elschot*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文通过LoRA对MedSAM3进行参数高效微调，仅用10个标注病例即可在CT和MRI上实现与使用海量数据训练的专业分割系统（如TotalSegmentator、MRSegmentator）相近的性能，同时训练时间仅为nnU-Net的1/3左右。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学图像分割在临床中至关重要，但专业工具需要大规模标注数据训练，医学基础模型零样本性能有限；LoRA虽然实现高效参数适配，但尚不清楚需要多少专家标注病例才能达到临床可用的分割性能，这是本文要解决的关键问题。

Method: 使用LoRA对MedSAM3进行参数高效微调，针对CT和MRI中的五个腹部器官（肝、肾、脾、胆囊、胰腺），仅使用1、2、5、10个标注病例进行训练，并在AMOS22数据集上评估；同时外部验证扩展到Whole Heart Segmentation心脏分割数据集。

Result: 仅10个标注病例即可获得有竞争力的性能：胆囊分割达到Dice 0.68（CT）和0.59（MRI），而现有工具近乎失败（Dice 0.0004）；肝、肾、脾与MRSegmentator差距在5-10%以内但标注数据少100倍以上；心脏左心室分割也达到竞争性表现；训练仅需3-5小时/器官，比nnU-Net快2-3倍。

Conclusion: 十个标注病例足以实现临床有用的分割性能，有效缓解了图像标注和训练时间两个瓶颈，方法还能泛化到超出现有工具范围的临床应用（如心脏分割）。

Abstract: Medical image segmentation is essential for clinical workflows such as treatment planning and disease assessment. While specialist tools like TotalSegmentator and MRSegmentator achieve strong performance, they require large annotated datasets for training. Medical foundation models offer a promising alternative through large-scale pretraining that reduces the annotation burden for new tasks, but zero-shot performance remains limited. Parameter-efficient adaptation via Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) enables efficient specialization with few trainable parameters, but a key question remains: how many expert-annotated cases are needed to achieve clinically useful segmentation performance? We address this by adapting MedSAM3 with LoRA for five abdominal organs (liver, kidneys, spleen, gallbladder, and pancreas) in CT and MRI using only 1, 2, 5, and 10 annotated cases, evaluating on AMOS22 dataset. With just 10 cases, models achieve performance competitive with specialist systems trained on orders of magnitude more data. Notably, this includes reliable gallbladder segmentation (Dice 0.68 CT, 0.59 MRI) where existing tools fail almost completely (Dice 0.0004), while remaining within 5--10% of MRSegmentator for liver, kidneys, and spleen using over 100 times fewer annotations. Furthermore, external validation on the Whole Heart Segmentation dataset shows that the approach extends to cardiac segmentation, a use case beyond the scope of TotalSegmentator (MRI) and MRSegmentator, achieving competitive left ventricle (LV) performance with only 10 annotated cases. Training requires only3--5,hours per organ on a single GPU, approximately 2--3 times faster than nnU-Net. These findings suggest that ten annotated cases are sufficient for clinically useful segmentation, effectively reducing bottlenecks for both image annotation and training time.

</details>


### [56] [CL4D: Contrastive Language-4D Pretraining for Vision-Language Reasoning in Dynamic Scenes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18734)
*Kumal Hewagamage,Isuranga Senavirathne,Sasika Amarasinghe,Hasitha Gallella,Dulanga Weerakoon,Vigneshwaran Subbaraju,Ranga Rodrigo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了CL4D，首个直接作用于动态点云的4D视觉编码器，通过对比学习对齐时空几何表示与自然语言描述，并在此基础上构建4DVLM，这是首个无需2D图像/视频或静态3D点云、直接在4D点云上进行视觉语言建模的VLM。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉编码器要么局限于静态2D图像或3D点云（缺乏时间建模），要么局限于2D视频（缺乏精确的几何深度推理），无法同时捕捉动态场景中的空间结构和运动演化。这限制了具身AI智能体在动态物理环境中的4D理解与推理能力。

Method: 1) 提出CL4D，一个直接操作动态点云的4D视觉编码器，采用对比学习目标将时空几何表示与自然语言描述对齐到共享嵌入空间，实现零样本运动-文本双向检索。2) 在CL4D基础上构建4DVLM，一种直接以动态几何表示为条件的4D视觉语言模型。3) 构建了新数据集DynAction4D，涵盖多样人体运动及不同物体交互和场景环境，用于训练CL4D和4DVLM。

Result: 在多个4D人体动作基准上，CL4D达到最先进性能，相较先前方法提升约16.75%。4DVLM在相同场景下（4D点云vs RGB视频输入）优于Gemini和GPT-5等前沿视频VLM。

Conclusion: CL4D作为首个基础级4D视觉编码器，证明了动态点云可以直接作为4D视觉语言理解的基础表示，无需依赖2D图像或视频输入。4DVLM进一步验证了4D点云在视觉语言建模中的优越性，为具身AI在动态环境中的感知与推理提供了新方向。

Abstract: 4D understanding and reasoning is a fundamental capability for embodied AI agents operating in dynamic physical environments. However, existing vision encoders are largely limited to static 2D images or 3D point clouds without temporal modeling, or to 2D videos that lack accurate geometric depth reasoning. Consequently, current approaches fail to jointly capture spatial structure and motion evolution in dynamic scenes. We present CL4D, the first foundational 4D vision encoder that directly operates on dynamic point clouds, trained with a contrastive learning objective to align spatio-temporal geometric representations with natural language descriptions. By learning a shared embedding space between text and 4D scene dynamics, CL4D enables zero-shot motion-to-text and text-to-motion retrieval in dynamic environments and serves as a foundational 4D vision encoder for downstream 4D vision-language tasks. Building on this encoder, we introduce 4DVLM, a 4D vision-language model that conditions language generation on dynamic geometric representations. 4DVLM is the first VLM designed to operate directly on 4D point clouds without relying on 2D images, 2D videos, or static 3D point clouds. We train CL4D and subsequently 4DVLM on a newly constructed dataset termed DynAction4D capturing diverse human motions across varying object interactions and scene environments. Extensive experiments across multiple 4D human action benchmarks demonstrate that CL4D achieves state-of-the-art performance, with improvements of approximately ~16.75% over prior methods. Furthermore, 4DVLM outperforms frontier video VLMs such as Gemini and GPT-5 even when these models are provided with RGB video sequences corresponding to the same scenes represented as 4D point clouds for 4DVLM.

</details>


### [57] [SED-FOD: Scattering-Aware Expert Decomposition for Few-Shot Cross-Sensor SAR Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18755)
*Shu Yang,Zhen Chen,Zhiyu Jiang,Yanlei Li,Xingdong Liang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种散射感知的共享/特定特征分解框架（散射感知共享-特定特征分解框架），用于少样本SAR域自适应目标检测，通过共享路径学习可迁移的物体结构信息并进行非对称域对齐，同时利用软化门控的散射特定专家路径自适应补偿异构SAR响应，在跨域少样本检测任务上取得了优异性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SAR目标检测在跨域场景下性能严重退化，因为频段、分辨率、背景杂波和目标散射响应等差异导致域间分布不匹配。现有域自适应方法主要追求域不变特征对齐，却抑制了对目标检测有用的传感器相关散射特征。这一挑战在少样本场景下更加严峻，因为仅有少量带标注的目标域SAR图像可用。

Method: 提出散射感知共享-特定特征分解框架：将检测特征分解为共享路径和多个软化门控的散射特定专家路径。共享路径学习可迁移的物体结构信息，用于非对称域对齐；散射特定专家路径自适应补偿异构SAR响应。同时引入路由域辅助损失以鼓励特定专家捕获传感器相关的路由偏好，以及专家平衡损失防止路由坍缩。

Result: 在FARAD-X/FARAD-Ka和MiniSAR之间的四个双向异构SAR检测任务上，在不同少样本设置下进行了广泛实验，结果表明所提方法在正向和反向自适应方向上均取得了优越性能。

Conclusion: 通过显式区分可迁移的共享特征与传感器相关的散射特定特征，所提框架有效解决了少样本SAR域自适应目标检测中的域偏移问题，同时保留了对检测有用的散射信息，在两个方向的域适应任务上均表现出色。

Abstract: Synthetic aperture radar (SAR) object detection is an important part of remote sensing interpretation. However, because of variations in frequency band, resolution, background clutter, and target scattering responses, the performance of existing detectors often degrades when training and testing data are acquired from different SAR domains. Although domain adaptation methods offer a promising paradigm for solving this problem, most of them mainly pursue domain-invariant feature alignment and suppress sensor-dependent scattering characteristics that are useful for object detection. This problem becomes more challenging in few-shot scenarios, where only a few fully annotated target-domain SAR images are available. To address this issue, we propose a scattering-aware shared-specific feature decomposition framework for few-shot SAR domain adaptation object detection. We decompose detection features into a shared path and several soft-gated scattering-specific expert paths. The shared path learns transferable object structural information and is used for asymmetric domain alignment, while the scattering-specific experts adaptively compensate heterogeneous SAR responses. In addition, routing-domain auxiliary loss is introduced to encourage specific experts to capture sensor-dependent routing preferences, and an expert balancing loss is used to prevent routing collapse. Extensive experiments on four bidirectional heterogeneous SAR detection tasks between FARAD-X/FARAD-Ka and MiniSAR under different few-shot settings have been conducted and experimental results demonstrate that the proposed method achieves superior performance in both forward and reverse adaptation directions.

</details>


### [58] [MIFR: A Modality-Invariant and Fair Representation Framework for Skin Disease Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18774)
*Asonyu Senge Njih,Yvan Guifo Fodjo,Vianney Kengne Tchendji,Jerry Lacmou Zeutouo,Kerol Djoumessi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出MIFR（模态不变公平表示）框架，通过配对临床照片与皮肤镜图像，结合五组件多目标损失（加权交叉熵、混淆损失、皮肤类型分类损失、监督对比损失、模态不变损失），解决皮肤病分类中单一模态依赖和跨肤色性能差异两大问题。在HIBA+Derm7pt及外部数据集上获得与基线相当的预测性能和公平性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 皮肤病是重大全球公共卫生负担，但现有机器学习诊断工具存在两个关键缺陷：仅依赖单一模态进行诊断，以及不同肤色之间存在系统性性能差异。已有方法分别解决上述两个问题，但缺乏统一框架同时处理模态依赖和公平性问题。

Method: 提出MIFR框架：使用基于ViT的编码器配对临床照片与皮肤镜图像，通过模态特定投影头将输入映射到高维嵌入空间；采用五组件多目标损失函数，包括用于分类的加权交叉熵、用于公平性的混淆损失和皮肤类型分类损失、用于类别对齐的每模态监督对比损失，以及用于临床与皮肤镜模态对齐的模态不变损失。

Result: 在HIBA+Derm7pt配对数据集和外部PAD-UFES-20、ISIC 2019数据集上的实验表明，模态不变表示学习在预测性能上与相关基线模型相当，在内部数据集上公平性也具有竞争力。t-SNE可视化确认了同一疾病的临床和皮肤镜嵌入在几何上对齐。

Conclusion: 模态不变表示学习能够同时缓解单一模态依赖和肤色公平性问题，提供与基线相当的预测性能与公平性，并通过嵌入空间对齐验证了联合目标的合理性。

Abstract: Skin diseases represent a major global public health burden, yet machine learning tools developed to assist in their diagnosis suffer from two critical limitations: reliance on only one modality for diagnosis and systematic performance disparities across skin tones. While existing approaches address each challenge separately, this work proposes a modality-invariant framework with fair representation (MIFR) for skin disease classification. The architecture pairs clinical photographs with dermoscopic images using ViT-based encoders, projecting each input into a high-dimensional embedding space via modality-specific projection heads. The resulting model is trained with a five-component multi-objective loss including weighted cross-entropy for classification, confusion and skin-type classification losses for fairness, per-modality supervised contrastive loss for class alignment, and a modality-invariance loss for clinical and dermoscopic modality alignment. Experiments on the HIBA+Derm7pt paired dataset and the external PAD-UFES-20 and ISIC 2019 datasets showed that modality-invariant representation learning provides competitive predictive performance compare to relevant baseline models and competitive fairness on the internal dataset. t-SNE visualizations confirmed that clinical and dermoscopic embeddings of the same disease are geometrically aligned, validating the joint objectives.

</details>


### [59] [EfficientSync: Real-Time Lip Synchronization via Deformation-Based Reference Texture Mixing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18832)
*Fa-Ting Hong,Runzhen Liu,Luchuan Song,Hongmin Cai,Chuhua Xian*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: EfficientSync提出了一种基于形变的实时音频驱动口型同步框架，通过动态纹理混合器、时空位移自适应掩码和STAR采样三个模块，在保留参考帧真实纹理的前提下实现166 FPS的高保真口型同步，优于现有GAN/扩散方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有音频驱动口型同步方法（GAN/扩散解码器）重建整个下脸区域，不仅延迟高，还会产生牙齿、唇纹等口腔内部细节的幻觉，无法保留真实纹理。作者认为身份保持的瓶颈不在于参考帧不足，而在于缺乏能够忠实迁移参考帧中所含真实纹理的机制。

Method: 提出三个核心模块：(1) 动态纹理混合器（Dynamic Texture Mixer）将多参考帧融合重构为通道级选择，在全局上下文中评估每个空间对齐的参考帧，并通过通道级加权求和聚合，以低成本保持纹理完整性；(2) 时空位移自适应掩码（Spatio-Temporal Shifted Adaptive Masking）将源帧分解为唇部生成条件和独立背景先验，抑制下脸泄漏同时将合成嘴部无缝融入背景；(3) STAR采样作为零开销预处理步骤，检索最清晰且拓扑多样性最强的参考帧。

Result: 在HDTF和VFHQ数据集上达到最先进的视觉质量和身份保持效果，在单GPU上实现166 FPS的实时推理速度。

Conclusion: 通过保留而非重建参考纹理的形变策略，EfficientSync证明了在保持高身份保真度的同时实现实时口型同步是可行的，为音频驱动口型同步提供了兼顾效率与真实性的新范式。

Abstract: Audio-driven lip synchronization manipulates the mouth region of a talking-face video to match the driving audio while preserving head pose, identity, and background. Although the task is inherently local editing, prevailing approaches reconstruct the entire lower face with heavy GAN- or diffusion-based decoders, incurring substantial latency and, more critically, hallucinating intra-oral details such as teeth and lip wrinkles instead of preserving authentic textures. We contend that the bottleneck in identity preservation is not the scarcity of reference frames, but the lack of a mechanism that faithfully transfers the genuine textures they already contain. We therefore present EfficientSync, a real-time deformation-based framework that retains reference textures rather than resynthesizing them. First, the Dynamic Texture Mixer reformulates multi-reference fusion as channel-wise selection, evaluating each spatially aligned reference in a global context and aggregating them by channel-wise weighted summation, preserving textural integrity at low cost. Second, Spatio-Temporal Shifted Adaptive Masking decomposes the source frame into lip-generation conditions and an independent background prior, suppressing lower-face leakage while blending the synthesized mouth seamlessly into the background. Third, STAR Sampling, a zero-overhead pre-processing step, retrieves the sharpest and most topologically diverse reference frames. Experiments on HDTF and VFHQ show state-of-the-art visual quality and identity preservation at 166 FPS on a single GPU. Video demos: https://alunaticat.github.io/EfficientSync/index.html.

</details>


### [60] [EVADE: Evidence-Verified Agentic Diagnosis with Escape](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18833)
*Mohaimenul Azam Khan Raiaan,Nur Mohammad Fahad*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: EVADE是一种无需训练的推理方法，通过在不同图像视图间进行一致性门控验证来增强单一冻结医学VLM的部署安全性，在VQA-RAD、SLAKE和PathVQA上相比zero-shot将ECE降低多达45%，是唯一同时改善校准和选择性风险并保持准确率的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学视觉语言模型虽然准确率高但不可靠：系统性地过度自信、无法从测试时推理中获益、且缺乏对自身响应可信度的可靠校准能力，这在医疗部署场景中构成安全隐患。

Method: EVADE采用推断式非训练方法：(1)模型先对全图作答；(2)当不确定时，定位最具诊断相关性的区域；(3)对放大视图重新作答；(4)仅当全图响应与放大视图响应一致时才提交答案，否则弃权（abstain）。核心思想是通过跨图像视图验证门控一致性，而非让模型重读自身文本，以直接解决单模型自检中的验证幻觉问题。

Result: 在VQA-RAD、SLAKE和PathVQA上使用Qwen2.5-VL-7B的实验表明，EVADE是唯一同时改善校准和选择性风险并保持准确率的方法，ECE比zero-shot降低最多45%。对比之下，CoT、self-consistency和self-verification均至少在一个维度上失败。定位分析显示自提议区域在诊断结构定位上优于中心裁剪或随机裁剪，但7B模型无法利用该定位信息来修正答案，可靠性提升主要来自一致性门控和校准弃权。

Conclusion: 跨视图一致性门控与校准化弃权是提升医学VLM可靠性的有效途径，优于基于文本自读的自验证方法。但当前7B模型的定位能力尚不足以实现诊断引导的答案修正，未来需探索更强模型或更直接的定位-推理融合机制。

Abstract: Medical vision-language models (VLMs) can achieve high accuracy but remain unreliable: they are systematically overconfident, benefit little from test-time reasoning, and lack the ability to reliably calibrate trust in their own responses. We introduce EVADE (Evidence-Verified Agentic Diagnosis with Escape), an inferential, non-training method that enhances the safety of deploying a single frozen VLM. EVADE responds and, when uncertain, localises the region most diagnostically relevant, re-answers on a zoomed view, and commits only when both the entire image and the zoomed view responses agree; otherwise, it abstains. To directly address verification hallucination in single-model self-checking, our main idea is to verify gate consistency across different image views rather than re-reading the model's own text. Experimental evaluation on VQA-RAD, SLAKE, and PathVQA using Qwen2.5-VL-7B reports that EVADE is the only method that simultaneously improves both calibration and selective risk while maintaining accuracy, reducing expected calibration error (ECE) by up to 45% compared to zero-shot. Chain-of-thought, self-consistency, and self-verification all fail at least one axis. A grounding analysis reports that self-proposed regions perform better at diagnostic structure localisation than centres or random crops. However, a 7B VLM cannot use this localisation to revise answers. Therefore, reliability gains come from the consistency gate and calibrated abstention.

</details>


### [61] [RVLoss: Runoff Vote Loss for Self-Supervised LiDAR Scene Flow Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18864)
*Shiming Wang,Liangliang Nan,Julian Kooij,Holger Caesar,Yancong Lin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出RVLoss，一种通过两阶段runoff投票机制将运动刚性强约束嵌入自监督损失的LiDAR场景流估计方法，在Argoverse2 2026挑战赛中超越基线20%，并在四个跨数据集上均表现一致提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自监督LiDAR场景流方法主要依赖Chamfer损失（最近邻距离），但最近邻搜索不强制运动刚性，导致同一物体实例内的流不一致。已有方法通过额外正则项缓解该问题，但对大物体的流一致性改善仍然有限。

Method: 通过观察发现：基于最近邻搜索计算的逐点运动可被top-k投票聚合成少量主导流候选；当用这些候选补偿源点云时，能最佳代表底层刚体运动的流在第二轮投票（top-1投票）中获得最高共识。基于这一两阶段runoff投票机制，作者设计损失函数，生成聚类级刚性流和自由形式流作为伪标签用于自监督学习。RVLoss可无缝集成到现有前馈网络架构中。

Result: 在Argoverse2 2026挑战赛中，使用RVLoss训练的模型在自监督方法中达到最先进性能，优于其他损失设计的基线模型20%；跨数据集评估表明在四个额外数据集上均有一致的性能提升。

Conclusion: RVLoss通过两阶段runoff投票机制将运动刚性约束内嵌到损失设计中，有效提升了自监督LiDAR场景流的点级一致性，尤其在大型物体上表现突出，且具有通用性和跨数据集泛化能力。

Abstract: LiDAR scene flow estimates point-wise motion between two consecutive scans, referred to as the source and target. Leading self-supervised methods typically minimize the Chamfer loss, the nearest neighbor distance between the flow-compensated source and the target. However, nearest-neighbor search does not enforce motion rigidity, often leading to inconsistent flows within object instances. Existing approaches address this issue with additional regularization terms, but flow consistency among points remains limited, especially for large objects. We propose RVLoss, a self-supervised loss that incorporates motion rigidity by design through a runoff vote mechanism. Our key observation is that the point-wise motion, calculated from nearest neighbor search, can often be grouped into a small set of dominant flow candidates by voting (top-k voting). Furthermore, when compensating the source by these candidates, the flow that best represents the underlying rigid motion often yields the highest consensus after a second voting (top-1 voting). Based on this insight, we incorporate the two-stage runoff vote into loss design and create cluster-wise rigid flows and free-form flows as pseudo-labels for self-supervised learning. RVLoss can be seamlessly integrated into existing feedforward architectures. Experiments on the Argoverse2 2026 Challenge show that models trained with RVLoss achieve state-of-the-art performance among self-supervised approaches, outperforming baseline models trained with alternative loss designs by 20%. Moreover, cross-dataset evaluations demonstrate consistent performance improvements across four additional datasets. Code will be released upon acceptance.

</details>


### [62] [Falcon Perception-HD: High Density Perception via Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18881)
*Sofian Chaybouti,Yasser Dahou,Ngoc Dung Huynh,Reda Alami,Hilde Kuehne*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文探索使用强化学习（GRPO）对自回归感知模型进行后训练，直接优化评估指标（精确率/召回率），替代传统的SFT代理目标。在Falcon Perception基础上，设计了针对感知任务的结构化奖励和多头采样控制机制，在密集场景（高达500个物体）、指代表达分割（PBench和SACO-Gold）和物体存在性判断（MCC）等任务上均取得显著改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自回归感知模型在开放词汇设置下通常使用SFT加最大似然训练，但其优化的逐token交叉熵是一种代理目标，与感知评估指标（精确率和召回率）根本不对齐。这种错位导致模型在密集场景中性能急剧下降或崩溃，且存在mask重复等问题。

Method: 1) 基于Falcon Perception构建RL（GRPO）后训练框架；2) 设计面向集合结构输出的奖励函数（仅惩罚假阴性和假阳性）；3) 实现多头采样控制以应对感知任务特有的挑战；4) 开发两种混合自标注管线，分别针对困难指代表达和密集场景；5) 通过RL训练消除对NMS和坐标去重的需求。

Result: RL训练在密集场景（500物体/场景）中达到SOTA性能；修复了mask重复问题，几乎完全消除了对NMS和坐标去重及相应超参数调优的需求；在所有难度级别的指代表达分割（PBench和SACO-Gold）上均有提升；在不训练负样本的情况下保留了物体存在性知识（MCC评估）。

Conclusion: 简单的奖励设计（惩罚FN和FP）配合GRPO即可有效将自回归感知模型与评估指标直接对齐，显著提升性能、效率和鲁棒性。模型权重已发布，数据集将公开。

Abstract: Autoregressive perception models trained to localize visual entities under the open-vocabulary setting are mostly trained using Supervised fine-tuning (SFT) with maximum likelihood, yet it optimizes a proxy objective (per-token cross-entropy) that is fundamentally misaligned with perception metrics such as precision and recall. In this paper, we explore post-training reinforcement learning (RL), specifically GRPO, to directly align these models with their evaluation metrics. Building up on the recently introduced Falcon Perception, we design an RL framework that addresses perception-specific challenges: reward design for set-structured outputs and multi-head sampling control. We discover multiple benefits from RL for perception: first, RL unlocks state-of-the-art performance in very dense scenes (up to 500 objects per scene), a regime where most existing systems degrade sharply or collapse; furthermore it fixes common issues in autoregressive perception models like mask repetitions and removes almost entirely the need for NMS and coordinate deduplication, which improve both performance and efficiency and remove the need for hyperparameters tuning; overall, we notice improvements on all levels of difficulties in referring expression segmentation (on PBench and SACO-Gold), and we find an elegant way to preserve the knowledge of whether an object exists or not (as evaluated by MCC) without training on negative samples. We show that a simple reward that penalizes false negatives and positives is sufficient. We develop two hybrid self-annotation pipelines, respectively tailored for difficult referring expressions and very dense scenes, and show their benefits on RL-training. Model weights are released as a Falcon Perception revision~\footnote{https://huggingface.co/tiiuae/Falcon-Perception}. Datasets will be published.

</details>


### [63] [Learning-State-Aware Dynamic Generative Data Augmentation on Small-Scale Datasets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18907)
*Ting Xiang,Chenxi Deng,Jinhui Zhao,Bingting Jiang,Ke Zhang,Changjian Chen,Zhuo Tang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种学习状态感知的动态生成式数据增强方法（LSADA），通过为每个样本构建学习状态来指导样本特定的增强强度，并采用解耦的数据增强与扩散融合策略，在保持类别语义的同时提升图像多样性，在多个自然图像和医学图像数据集上超越了现有最优方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 小样本图像分类受限于训练数据稀缺，生成式数据增强（GDA）是有效解决方案。但现有方法采用任务无关的增强策略，忽略了下游模型的需求；近期动态GDA方法虽引入模型反馈，仍难以可靠地确定样本特定的增强强度，也无法根据图像不同区域调整增强策略，难以平衡图像多样性和类别语义。

Method: LSADA为每个样本基于当前损失和损失下降率构建学习状态，并将其映射为样本特定的增强强度；同时引入解耦的数据增强与扩散融合策略，对类别相关区域施加受控强度的变换，并生成多样化的类别无关区域，通过渐进融合提升图像多样性同时保留类别语义。

Result: 在九个公开数据集上，LSADA在六个自然图像数据集上平均超过现有最优动态GDA方法4.5%，在三个医学图像数据集上平均超过2.5%。

Conclusion: LSADA通过学习状态感知的样本特定增强强度设定和区域级解耦增强融合策略，有效解决了动态GDA中增强强度难以确定和无法区域自适应的问题，在自然图像和医学图像分类任务中都取得了显著的性能提升。

Abstract: Small-scale image classification is often limited by the scarcity of training data. Generative data augmentation (GDA) based on pretrained generative models has emerged as an effective solution. However, existing methods rely on task-agnostic augmentation strategies that overlook downstream model needs. Although recent dynamic GDA methods incorporate model feedback to guide augmentation, they still struggle to reliably determine sample-specific augmentation strengths and adapt augmentation strategies to different image regions while balancing image diversity and class semantics.
  To address these issues, we propose learning-state-aware dynamic generative data augmentation (LSADA). Specifically, LSADA constructs a learning state for each sample based on its current loss and loss-decrease rate, which is then mapped to a sample-specific augmentation strength. Furthermore, LSADA introduces a decoupled data augmentation and diffusion fusion strategy that applies strength-controlled transformations to class-relevant regions and generates diverse class-irrelevant regions, progressively fusing them to improve image diversity while preserving class semantics. Experiments on nine public datasets show that LSADA outperforms the existing SOTA dynamic GDA method by an average of 4.5% on six natural image datasets and 2.5% on three medical image datasets.

</details>


### [64] [Simple, Safe, and Overlooked: Reclaiming Sustainable Domain Generalization with Statistical Color Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18915)
*Sebastian Doerrich,Francesco Di Salvo,Shyam Nandan Rai,Marco Lents,Christian Ledig*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Colorist是一种训练无关、完全可解释的数据增强策略，通过在RGB颜色空间中应用全局均值-标准差匹配来生成结构完整的域变异，在病理、外周血、皮肤科和视网膜等医学影像数据集上显著提升分布外泛化性能，同时避免了深度生成模型的幻觉问题和高计算成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学图像分类器在开发与部署之间常因硬件变化、颜色变异和患者特征改变而失效。现有方案中，标准颜色抖动多样性不足，而深度生成风格迁移算法会产生幻觉特征、破坏临床相关结构并浪费大量计算资源。

Method: 重新审视古典统计颜色匹配方法，将其重新定位为Colorist：一种直接在RGB颜色空间中应用全局均值-标准差匹配的数据增强策略。该方法无需训练、完全可解释，能安全生成结构完整的域变异。

Result: 在分布外的组织病理、外周血、皮肤科和视网膜数据集上，相比最先进的域泛化正则化方法，平衡准确率最高提升9%；相比未增强基线提升13%。同时保持结构保真度并最小化碳足迹。

Conclusion: 统计颜色匹配是一种被忽视的、安全且可解释的临床鲁棒性替代方案，优于深度生成架构，且能无缝集成到标准数据加载器中。

Abstract: Hardware shifts, color variations, and changing patient characteristics between development and deployment routinely break trained medical image classifiers. Existing remedies fall short: standard color jittering provides insufficient diversity, while deep generative style transfer algorithms hallucinate features, destroy clinically relevant structures, and waste massive compute resources. To address this, we revisit classical statistical color matching and repurpose it as Colorist, a highly efficient data augmentation strategy that applies global mean-standard deviation matching directly in the RGB color space. We demonstrate that this training-free, fully interpretable approach safely generates structurally intact domain variations, outperforming deep generative models in structural fidelity and color alignment. Across out-of-distribution histopathology, peripheral blood, dermatology, and retinal datasets, it improves balanced accuracy by up to +9% over state-of-the-art domain generalization regularizers and by +13% over an unaugmented baseline. Moreover, by avoiding neural networks in the augmentation loop, Colorist preserves anatomical structure, minimizes carbon footprint, and integrates seamlessly into standard dataloaders. Together, these findings establish statistical matching as a safe, interpretable, yet overlooked alternative to deep architectures for clinical robustness. Source code is available at https://github.com/sdoerrich97/colorist.

</details>


### [65] [Institutional Books - Visual Elements: An open-source pipeline for extracting, classifying, deduplicating, and captioning visual elements from digital book collections](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18957)
*Jimmy Mendez,Matteo Cargnelutti,David Lowry-Duda,Catherine Brobston,Salwa Ismail,Greg Leppert,Amanda Watson,Jonathan Zittrain*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一个开源的端到端管线，用于从历史图书收藏中检测、分类、去重和生成视觉元素的字幕，并发布了从哈佛图书馆数据集983,004册扫描卷中提取的2,260万个视觉元素的初始数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 历史图书收藏中包含丰富的视觉元素（插图、照片、版画、装饰艺术等），但在大规模数字化项目中常被忽视。现有OCR技术已标准化文本提取，但这些视觉组件蕴含的细微语境信息尚未被自动化文本提取工作流充分利用，因此需要专门的管线来挖掘这些视觉内容的潜在价值。

Method: 构建了一个端到端开源管线（Institutional Books - Visual Elements），涵盖四个核心环节：视觉元素的检测（detecting）、分类（classifying）、去重（deduplicating）和字幕生成（captioning）。该管线应用于包含983,004册扫描卷的Institutional Books: Harvard Library数据集，提取出2,260万个视觉元素。

Result: 发布了包含2,260万视觉元素的初始数据集，这些元素来自983,004册历史图书扫描卷；同时开源了完整的检测-分类-去重-字幕管线，支持从AI模型训练到数字人文学研究的多种计算访问用例。

Conclusion: 该工作有助于社区范围内的努力，通过计算手段访问数字化图书馆馆藏，为历史视觉材料开辟新的应用场景，弥补了大规模数字化项目中视觉内容未被充分利用的空白。

Abstract: Historical book collections contain rich visual elements - such as illustrations, photographs, engravings, and decorative art - that are frequently under-explored in large-scale digitization projects. While Optical Character Recognition (OCR) has standardized the extraction of textual content, these visual components offer a layer of nuance and context that remains largely untapped by automated text extraction workflows. This technical report introduces Institutional Books - Visual Elements, an open-source end-to-end pipeline for detecting, classifying, deduplicating, and captioning visual elements from historical book collections. Alongside this pipeline, we release an initial dataset of 22.6 million visual elements extracted from the 983,004 scanned volumes that comprise the Institutional Books: Harvard Library dataset. This work contributes to ongoing, community-wide efforts to enable new use cases for digitized library collections through computational access, from artificial intelligence model training to digital humanities research.

</details>


### [66] [Frozen DINO Localizes Image Edits Without a Localizer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18968)
*Zane Kumar,Vishal Jain,Bernhard Kainz*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出TRAIL方法，利用冻结DINO编码器的patch token余弦漂移来定位AI图像编辑区域。该方法无需训练，仅对图像施加一次全局Haar扰动，然后映射对应patch token之间的余弦漂移即可生成定位图。在CocoGlide测试集上达到0.903的patch AUROC，与掩码监督的Detective SAM（0.912）相当，且无需生成器即可迁移到Poisson图像插值任务。研究发现定位信号存在于DINO编码器的深层（归一化深度0.80-0.94），且全局上下文对定位效果至关重要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 局部图像编辑可以改变照片的含义而保留大部分真实内容，因此取证分析需要精确定位编辑发生的位置。现有的定位方法通常需要掩码监督训练，且依赖特定生成器知识，缺乏一种无需训练、不依赖生成器且能自动定位编辑区域的通用方法。

Method: TRAIL方法基于以下核心观察：冻结DINO编码器的patch-level扰动响应本身就是定位图。具体流程为：对图像施加一次全局Haar扰动，然后计算扰动前后对应patch token之间的余弦漂移，漂移较大的区域即为编辑定位区域。该方法无需任何训练，且可在不同DINO编码器间迁移。

Result: 在80张源分离的CocoGlide测试图像上，TRAIL达到0.903的patch AUROC（对比掩码监督的Detective SAM为0.912）；固定阈值Dice为0.619（对比0.709），使用oracle阈值可将TRAIL提升至0.790。迁移到Poisson图像插值任务时，TRAIL达到0.855 AUROC（对比0.864），证明该线索不依赖生成器。对16种DINO编码器的分析表明，最佳层位于归一化深度0.80-0.94。全局上下文至关重要：从全局扰动（0.903 AUROC）降至局部画布内扰动（0.857）和独立编码裁剪（0.735）。

Conclusion: 冻结的DINO patch token包含强力的深层定位信号，其可见性取决于扰动方式和保留的全局上下文。TRAIL提供了一种无需训练、无需生成器知识的图像编辑定位方法，且定位效果接近掩码监督方法，为图像取证提供了一种高效且通用的新路径。

Abstract: Localized image edits can change a photograph's meaning while leaving most of it authentic, so forensic analysis must identify where an edit occurred. We show that patch-level perturbation responses from frozen DINO encoders are themselves localization maps.
